Writes the implementation with Reanimated, Gesture Handler, Expo Router and expo-haptics. Use when animating anything in an Expo app, adding gestures, sheets, screen transitions, press feedback or haptics, or fixing motion that stutters on device. For web animation use animate.

- Animation Performance (emilkowalski/animation-performance)
  page: /skills/emilkowalski/animation-performance
  llms: /skills/emilkowalski/animation-performance/llms.txt
  source: emilkowalski
  description: Keep web animations inside the frame budget — animate composite-only properties, keep motion off the main thread when the page is busy, and find the cause when an animation drops frames. Covers the layout/paint/composite pipeline, cheaper property swaps, CSS vs WAAPI vs requestAnimationFrame, Framer Motion's hardware-accelerated path, React re-rendering per frame, inherited CSS variable recalculation, blur cost, and when `will-change` actually helps. Use when an animation is janky, choppy, stutters, or drops frames; when choosing which property to animate; when deciding between CSS and JS animation on performance grounds; or when reaching for `will-change` or GPU layers. For choosing easing, duration, or whether to animate at all use animate; for reviewing motion as a whole use review-animations.

Read before authoring any transition, text-beat handoff, kinetic text entry, or group reposition. accessibility addyosmani

Regression check that diffs a live page's accessibility violations against a baseline from uncommitted changes or a branch.

Run the AccessLint rule engine against a live page and return a worklist of WCAG 2.2 violations grounded to DOM selectors and source locations.

accessible-animation iart-ai

Tiered reduced-motion patterns for CSS, GSAP, Framer Motion, Lenis, and other web animation systems.

adapt pbakaus

ai-output-judgement kemiljk

Make crafted, highly detailed isometric SVG assets for landing pages, product sites, launch posts, social cards and decks — a hero object, a set of spot illustrations, a feature-tile scene — drawn as real, well-made physical things with many small precise solid parts, four-tone shading, correct painter's order and calm CSS-only ambient motion, mostly monochrome with brand accents. Use this when someone wants a nice-looking illustration, hero image, product visual, spot illustration, icon set, isometric artwork, "Linear-style" or "premium" drawing, or a visual for a section of a site, where the goal is a striking, on-brand picture rather than a diagram that explains how something works. Ships a standalone .svg that works as, inline or in design tools, plus a preview page; can add WebGL shaders or the 3D mode when asked. For interactive figures that explain a technical concept with a live readout, use the anatomy skill instead.

animate emilkowalski

Writes the implementation. Use when asked to animate something, add motion, make a component feel alive, or build a transition. For critiquing existing motion use review-animations; for auditing a whole codebase use improve-animations.

Use when animating anything in an Expo app, adding gestures, sheets, screen transitions, press feedback or haptics, or fixing motion that stutters on device. For web animation use animate.

animation-performance emilkowalski

Keep web animations inside the frame budget — animate composite-only properties, keep motion off the main thread when the page is busy, and find the cause when an animation drops frames. Covers the layout/paint/composite pipeline, cheaper property swaps, CSS vs WAAPI vs requestAnimationFrame, Framer Motion's hardware-accelerated path, React re-rendering per frame, inherited CSS variable recalculation, blur cost, and when `will-change` actually helps. Use when an animation is janky, choppy, stutters, or drops frames; when choosing which property to animate; when deciding between CSS and JS animation on performance grounds; or when reaching for `will-change` or GPU layers. For choosing easing, duration, or whether to animate at all use animate; for reviewing motion as a whole use review-animations.

cut-the-curve heygen-com

The technique catalog: five velocity-matched SEAMS (zoom-through, INVERSE zoom-through, cut-the-curve, waterfall cut, rack-focus blur-cut) plus the two in-scene techniques — waterfall ENTRY (staggered arrival cascades for title cards / segment openers) and the nudge curve (slow-fast-slow three-phase group slides). Covers partial-travel (~12% of frame) velocity matching via mirrored power4 eases, the Z scale-sign rule, size-scaled blur (10px text / 18-20px full-frame), word-by-word staggered cuts, cascade pacing by element weight, and the 10/65/25 slide ratio. Read before authoring any transition, text-beat handoff, kinetic text entry, or group reposition. motion-doctrine heygen-com

GATEWAY — load FIRST before composing any HyperFrames animation or video. The high-level motion law that makes a multi-scene video feel like ONE continuous camera move instead of a stack of independently-animated slides. Covers the vector law (how you exit determines how you enter, incl. the Z scale-sign rule), the film's current, carrier elements, causal motion, the Seam Gate (build-gate enforcement), the ban on idle wobble (motion must PERFORM, not breathe), stillness-before-climax, and the sustained-motion routes. Routes to the low-level technique skills (cut-the-curve — the full catalog incl. waterfall entry + nudge curve, oversized-cursor, seam-craft). These rules SUPERSEDE generic / upstream motion guidance. [continuity, direction, vector, momentum, seam, transition, ease, performance, idle-motion, narrative-motion, film-grammar]

Load when assembling the master timeline / index.html, when a white flash appears at a cut or crossfade seam (especially on dark films), when reasoning about why a transition opacity dip shows through, or when verifying the render-side mechanics of how overlapping scene wrappers blend. Does NOT contain the per-transition catalog — see the transition registry for individual transition entries.

Make any UI appear as if an AI agent is generating it. A dotted wireframe is drawn from the element's own structure (outline, boxes, text lines, icon paths, with guides, corner handles and size labels), then the real UI is revealed behind a scan line with a fade on a smooth ease-out. Two modes, skeleton and skeleton + generation texts (an agent log, the motion CSS being written, and a step line). One dependency-free runtime that works with any framework (vanilla, React, Next.js, Vue, Svelte). Use when the user asks for a "generation animation", "AI generating the UI", "agent building the component", "build-in animation", "wireframe to UI reveal", "skeleton generation", "dotted wireframe", "matrix dots", "make it look generated", "futuristic reveal", "generative UI loading", or for an AI product that should show its output being built. Also the commands transitions generate, transitions generate texts and transitions generate preview.

triage mattpocock Moves issues through a structured triage state machine.

tsdown rolldown

Bundle TypeScript and JavaScript libraries with tsdown, including declarations and multi-format builds.

turborepo vercel

Turborepo monorepo build system guidance for pipelines, caching, filtering, CI, and package boundaries.

typeset pbakaus

Improve typography systems, hierarchy, readability, and text cadence for more intentional interfaces.

ui-review arla6ka

Critiques a screen, flow, prototype or running build against agreed criteria, accessibility included, and ranks findings by severity. Use for "check this page before I ship", "is this flow ready", "is it accessible", keyboard or screen-reader checks, "it feels off on my phone", "it feels janky", or a second read on UI. A whole-app ship check goes to design-system-boss, token compliance to token-mapping.

ui-skills-root ibelick

Use when the user needs UI help and you must route by topic, stack, and intent to the smallest useful set of UI skills.

ui-styling nextlevelbuilder

Create beautiful, accessible user interfaces with shadcn/ui components (built on Radix UI + Tailwind), Tailwind CSS utility-first styling, and canvas-based visual designs. Use when building user interfaces, implementing design systems, creating responsive layouts, adding accessible components (dialogs, dropdowns, forms, tables), customizing themes and colors, implementing dark mode, generating visual designs and posters, or establishing consistent styling patterns across applications.

ui-ux-pro-max nextlevelbuilder

Comprehensive UI/UX design intelligence with 50+ styles, 97 palettes, and 9 technology stacks for building professional interfaces.

understand Egonex-AI

Analyze a codebase to produce an interactive knowledge graph for understanding architecture, components, and relationships

understand-chat Egonex-AI

Use when you need to ask questions about a codebase or understand code using a knowledge graph

understand-dashboard Egonex-AI

Launch the interactive web dashboard to visualize a codebase's knowledge graph

understand-diff Egonex-AI

Use when you need to analyze git diffs or pull requests to understand what changed, affected components, and risks

understand-domain Egonex-AI

Extract business domain knowledge from a codebase and generate an interactive domain flow graph. Works standalone (lightweight scan) or derives from an existing /understand knowledge graph.

understand-explain Egonex-AI

Use when you need a deep-dive explanation of a specific file, function, or module in the codebase

understand-figma Egonex-AI

Analyze a Figma file via the Figma REST API and generate an interactive design knowledge graph (pages, screens, components, component sets, instances, design tokens) with a kind:"design" dashboard.

understand-knowledge Egonex-AI

Analyze a Karpathy-pattern LLM wiki knowledge base and generate an interactive knowledge graph with entity extraction, implicit relationships, and topic clustering.

understand-onboard Egonex-AI

Use when you need to generate an onboarding guide for new team members joining a project

unlazy Leonxlnx

Anti-laziness execution discipline that finishes substantial tasks through gate files and runnable checks instead of premature done reports.

unocss antfu

UnoCSS atomic CSS engine guidance for rules, shortcuts, and presets like Wind, Icons, and Attributify.

unslop cursor

Cut AI tells from any writing by editing text to remove machine patterns and restore a more human voice.

use-of-color AccessLint

Compatibility listing for use-of-color installs from AccessLint's marketplace skill set.

variant jakubkrehel

Answers which of these rather than is this right. Builds several genuinely different versions of one piece of UI behind a picker in the real page, so you can flip between them and promote the one that wins.

react-best-practices vercel-labs

Vercel React best practices for rendering performance, bundle efficiency, and scalable component architecture.

video-interaction-mapper figma

Analyze a UI screen recording and map interaction states into an annotated Figma Design storyboard with extracted before/after frames.

video-shotcraft Vincentwei1021

Create cinematic product videos from shot recipe cards with Remotion, page screenshots, 2.5D camera moves, and beat-synced cuts.

visual-pr humanlayer

Create or update a pull request description that helps reviewers understand why the change exists and the shape of the implementation.

vite antfu

Vite configuration and plugin guidance, including SSR and Vite 8 Rolldown migration patterns.

vitepress antfu

VitePress documentation site guidance for configuration, theming, and Markdown plus Vue content.

vitest antfu

Vitest testing best practices for unit tests, mocking, coverage, fixtures, and test filtering.

vue antfu

Vue 3 Composition API and reactivity guidance for SFCs, script setup macros, and built-in components.

vue-best-practices vuejs-ai

Core Vue development best practices for component architecture, reactivity, and maintainable code.

vue-debug-guides vuejs-ai

Practical debugging workflows for diagnosing and fixing Vue reactivity, rendering, and state issues.

vue-jsx-best-practices vuejs-ai

Guidance for writing robust, type-safe, and readable Vue components using JSX.

Best practices for structuring and scaling Vue applications built with the Options API.

vue-pinia-best-practices vuejs-ai

Patterns for clean, scalable state management in Vue apps using Pinia stores.

vue-router-best-practices vuejs-ai

Routing architecture and navigation patterns for maintainable Vue Router applications.

vue-testing-best-practices vuejs-ai

Testing strategies for Vue applications, including component tests, integration tests, and reliability patterns.

vueuse-functions vueuse

Apply VueUse composables to build concise, maintainable Vue and Nuxt features.

wcag-audit-patterns wshobson

Conduct WCAG 2.2 accessibility audits with automated testing, manual verification, and remediation guidance. Use when auditing websites for accessibility, fixing WCAG violations, or implementing accessible design patterns.

web-clone Jane-xiaoer

网站复刻 / 克隆方法论。Use when the user says to clone, reproduce, or recreate a website or WebGL/Canvas/Three.js effect. Covers source-first reconstruction, repo scaffolding, reverse engineering, implementation paths, and verification for static sites, content sites, and heavy front-end experiences.

web-design-guidelines vercel-labs

Review UI code for Web Interface Guidelines compliance. Audit design, accessibility, and UX against Vercel's best practices.

web-perf elithrar

Diagnose page-load and interaction performance using browser traces, network evidence, and source code for Core Web Vitals and layout-shift investigations.

web-quality-audit addyosmani

Web quality auditing skill for Lighthouse-style analysis across performance, accessibility, best practices, and SEO signals.

webgl-components flornkm

Build small, always-on WebGL visuals (identity avatars, ambient orbs, glass and iridescent surfaces, animated textures) that ship inside a normal web app UI without wrecking performance, accessibility, SSR, or layout. Use whenever a shader-, GLSL-, or canvas-driven decorative element is added to a product UI, especially one rendered many times per page or per list; when reviewing or optimizing one; or when debugging one that renders blurry, aliased, all-black, or paints over the surrounding UI on machines without hardware acceleration.

webgl-landing-steering MengTo

Use when steering a landing page around a WebGL centerpiece or hero experience.

wireframe-portal-title heygen-com

A wireframe portal bursts open, the title comes through, then its letters swap into a second phrase. HyperFrames block, 1920×1080, 8s, 9 variables.

write-first-design kemiljk

Clarify uncertain product decisions in a short written brief before substantial design or implementation work.

write-swift emilkowalski

How to write modern Swift well — modeling with value types, Swift 6 data-race safety and approachable concurrency (@concurrent, main-actor-by-default, actors, task groups), protocols and generics (some vs any), API design, performance and ARC, Swift Testing, macros, and the modern language features agents don't know about yet. Use when writing, reviewing, or migrating Swift, or when a concurrency error, a hang, a data race, a retain cycle, or a performance problem needs fixing.

3dicon samyost1

Turn a prompt or still image into a looping animated icon with real transparency, output as animated WebP.

12-principles-of-animation raphaelsalaja

Apply Disney's 12 animation principles to web interfaces to make motion feel natural, organic, and human.

60fps-animation iart-ai

Web animation performance guidance for avoiding layout thrashing and reaching smooth 60/120fps motion with compositor-friendly techniques.

3.   CLI

# CLI

Browse and fetch UI Skills from your terminal. The CLI reads from the same registry as the website and the MCP server.

## Install

npx ui-skills

## Start here

Print the routing skill. It helps an agent choose the smallest useful skill for a task.

npx ui-skills start

## List categories

Print every category in the registry.

npx ui-skills categories

## List skills

Print every skill with its category and description.

npx ui-skills list

## Filter by category

Show only skills in one category, such as motion.

npx ui-skills list --category motion

## Get a skill

Print the full markdown for one skill.

npx ui-skills get baseline-ui

# MCP

Connect agents to the UI Skills catalog over Model Context Protocol. The server exposes the same registry as the `ui-skills` CLI.

## Endpoint

https://www.ui-skills.com/mcp

## Server card

https://www.ui-skills.com/.well-known/mcp/server-card.json

## Tools

list_skills

List skills from the registry. Optional query filter over path, name, or description.

get_skill

Fetch skill markdown by discovery name, slug, or pathSlug.

## Playbook — https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook
Title: Learn | UI Skills

URL Source: https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook

Markdown Content:
Interactive playground and lessons from the best design engineering skills

[Prevent layout shifts with aspect ratio Set `aspect-ratio` so nearby content does not jump while an image loads.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/reserve-space-with-aspect-ratio)[Balance heading text for easier reading Use `text-balance` for headings and `text-pretty` for body copy.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-text-balance)[Align numbers with tabular-nums Use `tabular-nums` to align numbers in tables and data-heavy UI.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-tabular-nums-for-data)[Use 44px touch targets for accessibility Give buttons and controls a touch target of at least 44 by 44px.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-large-touch-targets)[Match border radius on nested elements Set the nested element's radius to the parent's radius minus its padding. Formula: `inner radius = outer radius - padding`](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-concentric-border-radius)[Add scale feedback to pressed buttons Add subtle press feedback with `:active` and a scale near 0.96.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/add-scale-on-press)[Animate popovers from their trigger Animate each popover from the control that opened it.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/anchor-popovers-to-triggers)[Group content with spacing, not dividers Use spacing to group related content before adding divider lines.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/group-with-space-not-lines)[Preview the next item in scroll lists Show 16 to 32px of the next item to make horizontal scrolling clear.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/peek-the-next-scroll-item)[Use neutral borders around images Use a thin neutral outline instead of a tinted ring.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/outline-images-neutrally)[Truncate long titles in small spaces Use `line-clamp` or `truncate` when space is tight.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/clamp-overflowing-titles)[Improve contrast for muted text Give muted text enough color contrast to stay easy to read.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/keep-secondary-text-readable)[Avoid scale-zero entrance animations Start entrance animations near 95% size instead of scaling up from zero.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/avoid-entering-from-scale-zero)[Match loading skeletons to real content Match loading skeletons to the content they replace, and skip spinners when possible.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-structural-skeletons)[Limit each view to one accent color Use one accent per view. Keep secondary actions neutral.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/limit-accent-color-usage)[Keep body text lines easy to read Keep text lines between 60 and 75 characters for easier reading.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/cap-line-length)[Use shadows to show visual depth Use shadows to show depth. Use borders to show structure.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-shadow-for-elevation)[Add visible keyboard focus rings Use `:focus-visible` to keep keyboard focus clear and visible.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/show-visible-focus-rings)[Show form errors next to each field Put each validation error directly below its field.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/show-errors-beside-fields)[Add visible labels to every form field Use a visible label. Do not use placeholder text as the only label.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/label-every-form-field)[Give empty states one clear action Give an empty state one clear action, not just an explanation.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/give-empty-states-one-action)[Use sentence case for UI labels Write labels in sentence case, not all caps.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-sentence-case-labels)[Never use color alone for status Add text or an icon so status is never shown by color alone.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/pair-status-with-labels)[Use ease-out for entrance animations Use `ease-out` for entrances. `ease-in` feels slow when something opens.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-ease-out-on-enter)[Align icons by eye for better balance Nudge icons when geometric centering does not look visually balanced.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/align-icons-optically)[Match icon stroke weight to text Use a 2px stroke beside semibold text and a 1.5px stroke beside regular text.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/match-icon-stroke-weight)[Use outline icons for default states Use outline icons by default. Use a fill for the active state.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-outline-icons-by-default)[Avoid glow effects on primary buttons Avoid glow effects on primary buttons; use contrast and spacing instead.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/avoid-glow-primary-actions)[Animate icon state changes smoothly Cross-fade changing icons with opacity, scale, and blur for smoother feedback.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/animate-icon-state-changes)[Add space between nearby controls Leave about 12px between nearby bordered controls.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/give-targets-breathing-room)[Keep full-width buttons inside page margins Keep full-width buttons inside the page margins, not edge to edge.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/inset-primary-actions)[Use tighter line height for headings Use a line height near 1.1 for short headings.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/tighten-heading-line-height)[Adjust letter spacing for large text Large display text often needs slightly tighter letter spacing.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/tune-tracking-by-size)[Confirm destructive actions before they run Ask for confirmation before deleting data or taking an irreversible action.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/confirm-destructive-actions)[Fade the edges of scrollable lists Use a `mask-image` fade to show where a scrollable list continues.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/fade-scroll-edges)[Stagger hero animations on first load Stagger hero animations by about 100ms on first load, not during routine interactions.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/stagger-infrequent-entrances)[Keep exit animations short and subtle Keep exit animations short with a small `translateY` movement.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/keep-exits-subtle)[Show nearby tooltips faster Show nearby tooltips faster after the first tooltip opens.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/warm-toolbar-tooltips)[Use interruptible CSS transitions Use CSS transitions for interactive open and close states.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-interruptible-transitions)[Avoid repeated animations in routine UI Animate first-load entrances, but keep repeated actions like tab switches instant.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/restrain-high-frequency-motion)[Use ease-out instead of springs for feedback Use `ease-out` for toggles and toasts because springs can wobble.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-ease-not-spring-for-feedback)[Fade menus out when they close Close menus with a short fade. Do not move them far off screen.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/fade-menus-out)[Reserve brand colors for links and actions Keep headings neutral. Use the brand color for links and actions.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/reserve-brand-color-for-links)[Use solid modal backdrops instead of blur Use a solid modal backdrop instead of blur to avoid expensive full-screen rendering.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/use-solid-modal-scrims)[Fade overflowing text instead of clipping it Fade long labels instead of cutting them off with a hard ellipsis.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/fade-truncated-text)[Use matching enter and exit animations Use matching enter and exit animations so a surface feels connected.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/enter-and-exit-on-the-same-path)[Soften label changes with a short blur Add a short blur when labels change to avoid a distracting visual flash.](https://www.ui-skills.com/playbook/blur-imperfect-label-morphs)


## Design system (DESIGN.md) — https://www.ui-skills.com/design.md
---
version: alpha
name: UI Skills
description: Design language for UI Skills.
colors:
  page-default: "#FFFFFF"
  surface-default: "#FFFFFF"
  surface-subtle: "#F5F5F5"
  fill-default: "#FFFFFF"
  fill-subtle: "#FAFAFA"
  fill-strong: "#F5F5F5"
  fill-inverse: "#151515"
  fill-accent: "#E5F0FF"
  content-primary: "#151515"
  content-secondary: "#757575"
  content-muted: "#A5A5A5"
  content-extra-muted: "#E5E5E5"
  content-inverse: "#FFFFFF"
  content-accent: "#1F78FF"
  color-line-default: "#EDEDED"
typography:
  mono:
    fontFamily: JetBrains Mono
  sans:
    fontFamily: Inter Variable
  h1:
    className: type-h1
    fontFamily: Inter Variable
    fontSize: text-4xl
    fontWeight: 450
    lineHeight: leading-tight
    letterSpacing: tracking-tight
  h2:
    className: type-h2
    fontFamily: Inter Variable
    fontSize: text-xl
    fontWeight: 450
    lineHeight: leading-tight
    letterSpacing: tracking-tight
  bodyMd:
    className: type-body-md
    fontFamily: Inter Variable
    fontSize: text-sm
    fontWeight: 400
    lineHeight: leading-normal
    letterSpacing: tracking-normal
  bodySm:
    className: type-body-sm
    fontFamily: Inter Variable
    fontSize: text-xs
    fontWeight: 400
    lineHeight: leading-normal
    letterSpacing: tracking-normal
  code:
    className: type-code
    fontFamily: JetBrains Mono
    fontSize: text-xs
    fontWeight: 400
    lineHeight: leading-normal
    letterSpacing: tracking-normal
---

## Overview

Quiet, editorial, and code-first. UI Skills should feel like a precise developer tool, not a marketing site.

## Colors

- Use `page-default` for the page background and `surface-default` for standard surfaces.
- Use `surface-subtle`, `fill-subtle`, and `fill-strong` for restrained surface contrast.
- Use `content-primary`, `content-secondary`, `content-muted`, and `content-extra-muted` for text hierarchy.
- Use `color-line-default` for borders and dividers.
- Use `fill-inverse` and `content-inverse` for inverse controls and surfaces.
- Use `fill-accent` and `content-accent` for accent states.

## Typography

- Use Inter Variable for interface copy.
- Use JetBrains Mono for commands, code, and technical identifiers.
- Use `text-4xl`, weight 450, `leading-tight`, and `tracking-tight` for H1.
- Use `text-xl`, weight 450, `leading-tight`, and `tracking-tight` for H2.
- Use `text-sm`, weight 400, `leading-normal`, and `tracking-normal` for body md.
- Use `text-xs`, weight 400, `leading-normal`, and `tracking-normal` for body sm.
- Use `text-xs`, weight 400, `leading-normal`, and `tracking-normal` for code.
- Use sentence case for labels and links.

## Layout

- Use `container-7xl` for large page layouts and `container-3xl` for small page layouts.
- Both layout utilities center their content, fill the available width, and apply `px-5` horizontal padding.
- Use generous vertical spacing and light section boundaries.
- Use responsive grids for collections.
- Keep mobile padding consistent.
- Let long technical strings wrap or scroll.

## Elevation & Depth

- Use white surfaces with `shadow-2xs`.
- On larger screens, use `ring-1 ring-line-default` instead of visible borders.
- On mobile, use thin top and bottom borders for full-width surfaces.
- Keep elevation subtle.

## Shapes

- Use restrained rounded corners for cards, controls, dialogs, and code blocks.
- Keep long-form content rectangular.
- Use `fill-subtle` and `fill-strong` for hover states.

## Components

- Skill cards show the identifier, description, then source.
- Website buttons use three sizes: lg (`h-10 px-5 gap-2 rounded-xl`), md (`h-8 px-3 gap-1.5 rounded-lg`), and sm (`h-7 px-2 gap-1 rounded-lg`), all with `text-sm` and zero vertical padding.
- Website buttons use primary, secondary, and ghost variants. Primary uses `fill-inverse` with `content-inverse`; secondary uses `fill-default`, `line-default`, and `fill-subtle` on hover; ghost uses `fill-strong` and switches from `content-secondary` to `content-primary` on hover.
- Buttons use square corners by default and a full-radius rounded shape when requested.
- Code blocks use monospace text, clear surfaces, scrolling, and copy support.
- Search uses a focused dialog with keyboard navigation and a clear empty state.

## Do's and Don'ts

- Provide visible focus states.
- Respect reduced motion.
- Give empty states one clear action.
- Do not use glow, gradients, or decorative color when neutral styling is sufficient.

emilkowalski/animation-performance	https://raw.githubusercontent.com/emilkowalski/skills/main/skills/animation-performance/SKILL.md	Keep web animations inside the frame budget — animate composite-only properties, keep motion off the main thread when the page is busy, and find the cause when an animation drops frames. Covers the layout/paint/composite pipeline, cheaper property swaps, CSS vs WAAPI vs requestAnimationFrame, Framer Motion's hardware-accelerated path, React re-rendering per frame, inherited CSS variable recalculation, blur cost, and when `will-change` actually helps. Use when an animation is janky, choppy, stutters, or drops frames; when choosing which property to animate; when deciding between CSS and JS animation on performance grounds; or when reaching for `will-change` or GPU layers. For choosing easing, duration, or whether to animate at all use animate; for reviewing motion as a whole use review-animations.

One dependency-free runtime that works with any framework (vanilla, React, Next.js, Vue, Svelte). Use when the user asks for a "generation animation", "AI generating the UI", "agent building the component", "build-in animation", "wireframe to UI reveal", "skeleton generation", "dotted wireframe", "matrix dots", "make it look generated", "futuristic reveal", "generative UI loading", or for an AI product that should show its output being built. heygen-com/motion-doctrine	https://raw.githubusercontent.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/main/.agents/skills/motion-doctrine/SKILL.md	GATEWAY — load FIRST before composing any HyperFrames animation or video. Routes to the low-level technique skills (cut-the-curve — the full catalog incl. waterfall entry + nudge curve, oversized-cursor, seam-craft). These rules SUPERSEDE generic / upstream motion guidance. [continuity, direction, vector, momentum, seam, transition, ease, performance, idle-motion, narrative-motion, film-grammar]
heygen-com/oversized-cursor	https://raw.githubusercontent.com/heygen-com/hyperframes/main/.agents/skills/oversized-cursor/SKILL.md	House-style oversized macOS cursor technique for HyperFrames launch videos.
